	private void DoCommonDashHandle(out int dir, out bool dashing, DashStartAction dashStartAction = null)
	{
		dir = 0;
		dashing = false;
		if (dashTime > 0)
		{
			dashTime--;
		}
		if (dashTime < 0)
		{
			dashTime++;
		}
		int num = 0;
		bool flag = Settings.DashControl == Settings.DashPreference.AllowDoubleTap;
		if (controlDash && releaseDash)
		{
			int num2 = direction;
			int num3 = controlRight.ToInt() - controlLeft.ToInt();
			int num4 = num2;
			if (num3 == -num2)
			{
				num4 = num3;
			}
			num = num4;
		}
		if ((controlRight && releaseRight && flag) || num == 1)
		{
			if (dashTime > 0 || num == 1)
			{
				dir = 1;
				dashing = true;
				dashTime = 0;
				timeSinceLastDashStarted = 0;
				dashStartAction?.Invoke(dir);
			}
			else
			{
				dashTime = 15;
			}
		}
		else if ((controlLeft && releaseLeft && flag) || num == -1)
		{
			if (dashTime < 0 || num == -1)
			{
				dir = -1;
				dashing = true;
				dashTime = 0;
				timeSinceLastDashStarted = 0;
				dashStartAction?.Invoke(dir);
			}
			else
			{
				dashTime = -15;
			}
		}
		if (dashing && controlDash)
		{
			releaseDash = false;
		}
	}

Now I understand the dash structure:

**Vanilla dash model:**
- `dashDelay` is the master timer.
- If `dashDelay == 0` → `dash = dashType` (i.e., ready to dash again).
- If `dash == 0` → dashTime = 0, dashDelay = 0.
- Each tick, DashMovement calls DoCommonDashHandle which manages dashTime double-tap window (±15 ticks).
- When double-tap fires: `dir`, `dashing=true`, `dashTime=0`.
- For dash==1 (ninja): `velocity.X = 16.9 * dir` (single frame set), head-about-to-hit-wall → `velocity.X /= 2`, `dashDelay = -1`.
- While `dashDelay < 0` (i.e., -1): the `else` branch runs each tick with damping: `num11 = 12`, `num12 = 0.985` (for dash==1: `num12 = 0.985f; num14 = 0.94f; num15 = 20`).
  - If `velocity.X > 12 || velocity.X < -12` → `velocity.X *= 0.985`, return.
  - If `velocity.X > num13 || < -num13` where num13 = max(accRunSpeed, maxRunSpeed) → `velocity.X *= 0.94`, return.
  - else `dashDelay = num15` (= 20 for dash 1), and velocity.X set to ±num13 (i.e., normal run speed).
- So the dash is a single-frame 16.9 velocity impulse, then decays with 0.985 per tick while above 12, then 0.94 damping until below run speed, then 20-tick delay before next dash.

	public static Tile GetFloorTile(int x, int y)
	{
		Tile result = null;
		if (Main.tile[x - 1, y] == null)
		{
			Main.tile[x - 1, y] = new Tile();
		}
		if (Main.tile[x + 1, y] == null)
		{
			Main.tile[x + 1, y] = new Tile();
		}
		if (Main.tile[x, y] == null)
		{
			Main.tile[x, y] = new Tile();
		}
		if (Main.tile[x, y].nactive() && Main.tileSolid[Main.tile[x, y].type])
		{
			result = Main.tile[x, y];
		}
		else if (Main.tile[x - 1, y].nactive() && Main.tileSolid[Main.tile[x - 1, y].type])
		{
			result = Main.tile[x - 1, y];
		}
		else if (Main.tile[x + 1, y].nactive() && Main.tileSolid[Main.tile[x + 1, y].type])
		{
			result = Main.tile[x + 1, y];
		}
		return result;
	}

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-12T09:04:24.546Z · glm-x-preview-260804

```
There's `onManaSpent()` for nebula stacks — good place to also set manaRegenDelay. Both consumption sites should set `manaRegenDelay = maxRegenDelay` (vanilla ItemCheck_ApplyManaRegenDelay: manaRegenDelay = (int)maxRegenDelay, with spaceGun exception for items 127/4347/4348/514).

maxRegenDelay formula: (1 - statMana/statManaMax2) * 60 * 4 + 45, then *= 0.7. Note: this is computed each tick in vanilla before UpdateManaRegen (at line 24378-24379). Interesting: it uses CURRENT statMana — so when you spend mana, delay = (1 - mana/max)*240+45, ×0.7. E.g., full spend of 100/200: delay = (1-0.5)*240+45=165, ×0.7=115.5 → int 115.

Note vanilla computes maxRegenDelay in a different method (line 24378 — probably in JumpMovement or somewhere in ResetEffects region). Let me check the context of 24378 to know when it runs relative to UpdateManaRegen.

Actually let me check which method contains line 24378.
```

	public bool CheckMana(int amount, bool pay = false, bool blockQuickMana = false)
	{
		int num = (int)((float)amount * manaCost);
		if (statMana >= num)
		{
			if (pay)
			{
				statMana -= num;
			}
			return true;
		}
		if (manaFlower && !blockQuickMana)
		{
			QuickMana();
			if (statMana >= num)
			{
				if (pay)
				{
					statMana -= num;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
		return false;
	}

	private bool ItemCheck_CheckCanUse_CanPayMana(Item sItem, bool canUse)
	{
		GetItemManaUsageDetails(sItem, out var skipUsageCheck, out var rawAmountToPay, out var _);
		if (skipUsageCheck)
		{
			return canUse;
		}
		if (!CheckManaPredictWithoutUse(rawAmountToPay))
		{
			return false;
		}
		return canUse;
	}

	private bool ItemCheck_ActuallyPayMana(Item sItem)
	{
		GetItemManaUsageDetails(sItem, out var skipUsageCheck, out var rawAmountToPay, out var freeUsage);
		if (skipUsageCheck)
		{
			return true;
		}
		if (!CheckMana(rawAmountToPay, !freeUsage))
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

Key insight: `ItemCheck_ApplyManaRegenDelay` runs **every tick while itemAnimation > 0** (i.e., during the use animation of a mana item), setting `manaRegenDelay = (int)maxRegenDelay`. maxRegenDelay is computed each tick as (1 - mana/max)*240+45, ×0.7.

So the semantics: while you're using a mana item, delay continuously resets to maxRegenDelay (based on current mana fraction); after the animation ends, delay ticks down 1+bonus per tick (+1 if standing/grappling/manaRegenBuff, +0.05 arcane crystal). During manaRegenBuff, delay capped at 20. When delay > 0, manaRegen = 0.

Our model: we have useTime (cooldown) — an analog of useAnimation. Our magic use: `this.player.useTime = mg.speed` — vanilla mg.speed is useTime; useAnimation may differ. Hmm. We don't model itemAnimation separately. Simplification: treat useTime as the animation window (our useTime is set to mg.speed, i.e. useTime). In vanilla, itemAnimation = useAnimation which for most magic items = useTime... not always but close. Since our game has a single timer, I'll apply manaRegenDelay reset while useTime > 0 right after casting a mana item — that's the closest 1:1 available given our timer model. I'll mark with comment line numbers.

Since we set useTime = mg.speed right after the spend, setting delay each tick while useTime > 0 matches vanilla "every tick during itemAnimation".

But careful: useTime is also used as a generic action cooldown for other things (non-mana). Only apply when a mana item was used. 76	    const d = ITEM_DEFS[i];
77	    if (!d) continue;
78	    const vid = d.vid ?? (d.key.startsWith('vi_') ? parseInt(d.key.slice(3), 10) : -1);
79	    if (vid < 0) continue;
80	    const fx = FX[String(vid)];
81	    if (fx) m.set(i, fx);
82	  }
83	  return m;
84	})();
85	
86	export function accFxOfInternal(id: number): AccFx | null {

    //  无城镇 NPC 也会满地兔鼠(甚至刷到浮空岛——原版岛边 townNPCs=0 永不出小动物)
    this.spawnFriendlyCycle = false;
    if (!this.invaders && ((!bloodMoon) || dayTime) && !zoneEvil) {
      const deep = playerYpx / TILE > w.h - 200;   // :711 player.Center.Y/16 > UnderworldLayer
      if (deep) {
        // 地狱侧(:713-759):友好概率低
        if (townNPCs === 1) {
          if (this.rateRng() < 1 / 10) this.friendlyHit(); else spawnRate = Math.floor(spawnRate * 1.25);
        } else if (townNPCs === 2) {
          if (this.rateRng() < 1 / 5) this.friendlyHit(); else spawnRate = Math.floor(spawnRate * 1.5);
        } else if (townNPCs >= 3) {
          if (this.rateRng() < 1 / 3) this.friendlyHit(); else spawnRate = Math.floor(spawnRate * 2);
        }
      } else {
        // 地表/洞穴侧(:760-831):高友好率;townNPCs==0 → 永不友好(不出小动物)
        if (townNPCs === 1) {
          if (this.rateRng() < 1 / 3) this.friendlyHit(); else spawnRate = Math.floor(spawnRate * 2);
        } else if (townNPCs === 2) {
          if (this.rateRng() < 2 / 3) this.friendlyHit(); else spawnRate = Math.floor(spawnRate * 3);
        } else if (townNPCs >= 3) {
          this.friendlyHit();   // :824 非 ftw 恒友好(专家 1/30 例外未实装)
        }
      }
    }
    return { spawnRate, maxSpawns };
  }
  /** friendly 命中(:723/:740/:756/:804/:828):本仓签名限制,乘数由调用侧返回值体现——
   *  这里直接改 spawnFriendlyCycle;maxSpawns×0.5(地狱)/×0.6(地表) 简化统一按 0.6 */
  private friendlyHit(): void {
    this.spawnFriendlyCycle = true;
  }
  private rateRng(): number {
    return Math.random();
  }

  /** FindSpawnTile（L879-927）+ GetSpawnArea（L841-877）1:1：

   *  safeArea = 玩家 ±(0.52×全屏格)——生成点必须落在画面外（!safeArea.Contains）。
   *  viewHalfW/H = 半屏格数 → 0.7×全屏 = ×1.4 半屏；0.52×全屏 = ×1.04 半屏 */
  private findSpawnTile(rng: RNG, viewHalfW: number, viewHalfH: number): boolean {
    const st = this.world.store;
    const px = this.pX, py = this.pY;
    const rangeX = Math.max(1, Math.floor(viewHalfW * 1.4));
    const rangeY = Math.max(1, Math.floor(viewHalfH * 1.4));
    const safeX = Math.max(1, Math.floor(viewHalfW * 1.04));
    const safeY = Math.max(1, Math.floor(viewHalfH * 1.04));
    // WorldUtils.ClampToWorld
    const areaL = Math.max(2, px - rangeX), areaR = Math.min(st.w - 3, px + rangeX);
    const areaT = Math.max(2, py - rangeY), areaB = Math.min(st.h - 3, py + rangeY);
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 50; attempt++) {
      const tx = rng.int(areaL, areaR);
      let ty = rng.int(areaT, areaB);
      // L886-888：点在实心格 或 带房屋墙 → 重试（房屋内不刷怪的主守卫）
      if (st.isSolid(tx, ty)) continue;
      if (WALL_HOUSE.has(st.wall[st.idx(tx, ty)])) continue;
      this.skyMob = false;
      // L890-897：天空怪——高于 worldSurface×0.35 且露天（原版以"两侧 45% 带外"为条件，
      // 隐含不变量 = 浮空岛只在两侧、中央带无高空地面。此处改判"落脚面上方两格无墙"，
      // 在合法原版世界上与原版等价（岛皆露天），且兼容含中央浮空岛的存量世界——
      // 否则中央岛会被当地表刷僵尸/水母）

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-12T09:07:07.048Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
Full vanilla structure:
1. num = 4-6 + luck adds + expert + peddler's satchel + anniversary. (We do 4-6 + 4 luck rolls + expert; no satchel/anniversary — fine, note.)
2. If hardMode: first-item loop `while num2 < 5000 { num2++; AdjustSlotRarities(num2, ref rarity); GetItem(luck, rarity, ref it, 2); if canAdd { add; break } }`. Note: AdjustSlotRarities mutates `rarity` — since rarity = array (same ref), this mutation persists into subsequent loops? After this loop, the main loop uses `array` directly (`SetupTravelShop_GetItem(playerWithHighestLuck, array, ref it2)`) — array is the same object as rarity! So the rarity reductions from the first-item loop PERSIST into the item loop and the painting loop resets `rarity = array` too (same array). Hmm, so the rarity decay is sticky across the whole shop build. Important semantic! Since rarity == array (same reference), yes.

Wait — `int[] array = new int[6]{...}; int[] rarity = array;` then `rarity = array;` after loops — all the same array object. So AdjustSlotRarities decrements persist. That's the vanilla behavior (arguably a bug, but 1:1 = keep it).

3. Main item loop: `while (added < num) { GetItem(luck, array, ref it2); if canAdd → add }` — **no attempt cap** (unbounded!). If unlucky it could loop a lot; in practice rarity[0]=100 gives hits. Actually with no AdjustSlotRarities here, a run of misses is possible but bounded probabilistically. Note: GetItem with default minimumRarity=0.

4. Painting loop: `while num2 < 5000 { num2++; AdjustSlotRarities(num2, ref rarity); GetPainting(...); if canAdd → add, break }`.

Note also: the first-item loop only runs `if (Main.hardMode)` — in pre-hardmode, no first item with minRarity 2; the main loop just fills from 0.

So the fix for item 5:
```ts
private buildTravelStock() {
  const array = [100, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600];
  const rarity = array; // 同引用：AdjustSlotRarities 的降档全店持久（原版 rarity==array）
  const adjustSlotRarities = (slotItemAttempts: number) => {   // Chest.cs:919-947
    if (rarity[5] > 1 && slotItemAttempts > 4700) rarity[5] = 1;
    if (rarity[4] > 1 && slotItemAttempts > 4600) rarity[4] = 1;
    if (rarity[3] > 1 && slotItemAttempts > 4500) rarity[3] = 1;
    if (rarity[2] > 1 && slotItemAttempts > 4400) rarity[2] = 1;
    if (rarity[1] > 1 && slotItemAttempts > 4300) rarity[1] = 1;
    if (rarity[0] > 1 && slotItemAttempts > 4200) rarity[0] = 1;
  };
  ...
  // 首件（:1276-1299）：仅 hardMode；while num2<5000 + 每轮先降档 + minimumRarity=2
  if (this.world.flags.hardMode) {
    let num2 = 0;
    while (num2 < 5000) {
      num2++;
      adjustSlotRarities(num2);
      const it = pick(TRAVEL_POOL.items, 2);
      if (canAdd(it)) { stock.push(it); break; }
    }
  }
  // 主件环（:1300-1308）：added<num 无上限（原版无尝试上限）
  let guard = 0;
  while (stock.length < num) {
    const it = pick(TRAVEL_POOL.items, 0);
    if (canAdd(it)) stock.push(it);
    if (++guard > 50000) break; // 防死循环护栏（原版无上限，概率上必然终止）
  }
  // 画环（:1309-1321）：while num2<5000 重置计数 + 每轮先降档
  let num3 = 0;
  while (num3 < 5000) {
    num3++;
    adjustSlotRarities(num3);
    const it = pick(TRAVEL_POOL.paintings, 0);
    if (canAdd(it)) { stock.push(it); break; }
  }
}
```

Wait, careful with the painting loop in vanilla: `rarity = array; num2 = 0;` — rarity is re-assigned to array but it's the same array, so nothing is actually reset. num2 = 0 resets the attempt counter. So the painting loop's AdjustSlotRarities can lower rarity further (from where the first loop left it). OK so my structure with shared `rarity` array and separate counters is 1:1.

Also note our `pick()` filters `e.tier < minimumRarity` — need to verify our GetItem equivalent matches `minimumRarity <= N` semantics of vanilla. Vanilla GetItem: `if (minimumRarity <= 4 && RollLuck(rarity[4]) == 0) it = 3309;` etc. So items at tier 4 require minimumRarity<=4, i.e., tier>=minimumRarity. Our `e.tier < minimumRarity → skip` matches.

But there's a subtlety: vanilla GetItem checks `minimumRarity <= N` for N in 4,3,... and for tiers where minRarity > N it skips. With minimumRarity=2, tiers 0 and 1 are skipped. ✓ matches our `e.tier < 2` skip.

Also vanilla GetPainting — our paintings pool with tier. Fine.

One more check: in the first loop, `SetupTravelShop_GetItem(playerWithHighestLuck, rarity, ref it, 2)` — passes the (possibly degraded) rarity array. ✓ our pick uses rarity[].

Also the hardMode gating: our current code always does first item with minRarity 2 regardless of hardMode. Fix: only in hardMode. That's the "首件 minimumRarity=2 的 hardMode 门核对" the task mentions. ✓.

Also check `peddlersSatchelWasUsed` and anniversary — not in our game, skip with comment.

Now the `stock.length < num` loop: vanilla `added < num` where added increments only when item added (AddToShop skips 0 but canAdd already filtered). Note AddToShop for item 2260 adds 3 slots (2260,2261,2262) — the "Peddler's Satchel"? Actually 2260-2262 is "Advanced Combat Techniques"? Hmm — those are vanilla "Battle Cry"? Not important unless our pool contains 2260. Skip with comment.

Let me check TRAVEL_POOL definition to see the structure and whether `tier` and painting pools exist.
````

1	// 魔法投射物(原版 Projectile 14 Spark / 短命发光弹语义):
2	// 直线飞行、撞块消失、命中伤害、寿命短(原版 spark 30tick)。
3	import { Entity } from './Entity';
4	import { hitCritters, playEnemyHitSound } from './projTargets';
5	import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
6	import type { GameHooks } from './types';
7	import type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';
8	import type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';
9	
10	export class MagicProj extends Entity {
11	  w = 6; h = 6;
12	  vx: number;
13	  vy: number;
14	  damage: number;
15	  life: number;
16	  dead = false;
17	  color: string;
18	  grav = 0;
19	
20	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number, life: number, color: string) {
21	    super();
22	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
23	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
24	    this.damage = damage;
25	    this.life = life;
26	    this.color = color;
27	  }
28	
29	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
30	    // 世界坐标绘制(Renderer 实体循环运行在世界变换内,勿再自算屏幕坐标)
31	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
32	    if (!ctx) return;
33	    ctx.fillStyle = this.color;
34	    ctx.beginPath();
35	    ctx.arc(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
36	    ctx.fill();
37	  }
38	
39	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
40	    // 联机傀儡（远端弹幕快照驱动）：纯位移外推，不跑命中/碰撞（伤害归拥有者客户端结算）
41	    if (this.netPuppet) { this.netPuppetStep(); return; }
42	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
43	    this.x += this.vx;
44	    this.y += this.vy;
45	    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
46	    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
47	    if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) { this.dead = true; return; }
48	    // 小动物:一击致死并消耗弹体(近战挥砍同语义)
49	    if (hitCritters(this, game)) { this.dead = true; return; }
50	    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
51	      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean; dead: boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };
52	      if (e.dead) continue;
53	      if (this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y) {
54	        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
55	        e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game);
56	        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, this.damage, false, '#C080FF');
57	        this.dead = true;
58	        return;
59	      }
60	    }
61	  }
62	}
63

14.5 : 16.9) * dir;   // :21162 / :21131 单帧置速\n          // 头部将撞墙减半（:21132-21137）：身前半宽+2px 的头部点/身体中点格\n          // SolidOrSlopedTile → velocity.X /= 2\n          const wtx = Math.floor((this.cx + dir * (this.w / 2) + 2 * dir) / TILE);\n          const hty = Math.floor((this.cy + this.gravDir * (-this.h / 2) + 2 * this.gravDir) / TILE);\n          const solidOrSloped = (tx: number, ty: number): boolean =>\n            st.inBounds(tx, ty) && !!st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty)] && st.isSolid(tx, ty);\n          if (solidOrSloped(wtx, hty) || solidOrSloped(wtx, Math.floor(this.cy / TILE))) this.vx /= 2;\n          if (eq.dash === 2) { this.eocDash = 15; this.eocHitId = -1; } // :21170\n        } else {\n          this.dashT = dir * 15;        // :21316-21321 首击登记双击窗（dashTime ±15）\n        }\n      }\n      if (this.dashT > 0) this.dashT--; // :21268-21271 每帧向 0 收敛\n      else if (this.dashT < 0) this.dashT++;\n      if (this.dashDelay < 0) {\n        // 滑行阻尼（:20970-21113）：|vx|>12 → ×0.985；否则 |vx|>max(accRunSpeed,\n        // maxRunSpeed) → ×0.94；跌破档位即结束滑行（dashDelay=20）并把 vx 钳回\n        // ±maxRunSpeed（:21108-21113）。num13 = max(accRunSpeed,maxRunSpeed)\n        // （accRunSpeed=靴 6/裸 3，maxRunSpeed=3×moveSpeed）\n        const num13 = Math.max(eq.runSpeed > 0 ? eq.runSpeed : 3, 3 * eq.moveMult);\n        this.vortexStealthActive = false;  // :21106 滑行中强制显形\n        if (Math.abs(this.vx) > 12) {\n          this.vx *= 0.985;\n        } else if (Math.abs(this.vx) > num13) {\n          this.vx *= 0.94;\n        } else {\n          this.dashDelay = 20;             // :21110（dash1/2 档 num15=20）\n          this.vx = this.vx < 0 ? -num13 : num13;\n        }\n      }"
}

{
 "replace_all": false,

 "old_string": "  /** 旅行商人当期股票（Chest.SetupTravelShop :1237 结构 1:1）：\n   *  4-6 件商品（首件高稀有起步）+ 1 幅画；池内按原版 if 链顺序、后命中覆盖。\n   *  幸运度已接（Player.luck → Luck.RollLuck 三态，Luck.cs:5-16）：luck>0 时\n   *  RollLuck(N) 改掷 [N/2, N)（命中 0 的概率升 ~2 倍期望）、luck<0 改掷 [N, 2N)\n   *  （恒不中 0）；件数 num 的 RollLuck(4/8/16/32)==0 加件掷同链（SetupTravelShop :1251-1267）。\n   *  偏差（登记）：AdjustSlotRarities（Chest.cs:921-947，第 4200-4700 次尝试把 rarity\n   *  各档降 1）以\"失败渐放宽\"近似——原版 while 上限 5000 次与本仓 60 次上限不同尺度；\n   *  首件 minimumRarity=2 只在困难模式生效（:1277 hardMode 门）未区分 */\n  private travelStock: Array<[number, number]> = [];\n  private buildTravelStock(): Array<[number, number]> {\n    const rarity = [100, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600];\n    const luck = this.player.luck;   // Player.GetPlayerWithHighestLuck → 本地玩家（单人）\n    const rollZero = (n: number): boolean => rollLuck(luck, n) === 0;\n    const condOk = (c: string | null): boolean => {\n      const f = this.world.flags;\n      switch (c) {\n        case null: return true;\n        case 'hardMode': return !!f.hardMode;\n        case 'downedBoss3': return !!(f['downed_35'] || f.downedSkeletron);\n        case 'shadowOrbSmashed': return !!f.shadowOrbSmashed;\n        default: return false; // hardModeMech/moonlord/martians/frost/pirates/dontStarve 未达\n      }\n    };\n    const pick = (pool: Array<{ id: number; tier: number; cond: string | null }>, minimumRarity: number): number => {\n      let it = 0;\n      for (const e of pool) {\n        if (e.tier < minimumRarity || e.cond === 'skip' || !condOk(e.cond)) continue;\n        if (rollZero(rarity[e.tier])) it = e.id;  // SetupTravelShop_GetItem RollLuck(rarity[t])==0\n      }\n      return it;\n    };\n    const stock: number[] = [];\n    const canAdd = (id: number) => id > 0 && !stock.includes(id);\n    // 首件：minRarity=2 起步，失败渐放宽（AdjustSlotRarities 近似）\n    for (let tries = 0; stock.length < 1 && tries < 60; tries++) {\n      const it = pick(TRAVEL_POOL.items, tries > 40 ? - Probability multiplier: each tile visited every K rounds → per-visit prob = K × (per-round prob). Vanilla per-tick rate r_t; per-round (300 ticks) = 300·r_t; per-visit = K·300·r_t.

```ts
/** 树苗扫描切片计划（growSaplings 用，数值等价原版 WorldGen.UpdateWorld 全图均匀采样）：
 *  原版每 tick 在地下区采 (w×h)×1.5e-05 格（WorldGen.cs:71622-71631，j∈[worldSurface-1,
 *  h-20)）、地表区采 (w×h)×3e-05 格（:71549-71555，j∈[10, worldSurface-1)，雨天 ×1.5，
 *  :71557-71559）。本仓 300 tick 一轮、按列切片轮转全图：K 轮覆盖全图，每苗每轮被
 *  采到的概率 = K×(单轮概率)，单 tick 期望命中密度与原版逐点等价：
 *    p_tick = (K × 300 × rate × (w×h) / 区格数) / (K × 300) = rate × (w×h) / 区格数
 *  （rate=1.5e-05/3e-05，worldUpdateRate=1，Main.cs:734 默认） */
export function saplingScanPlan(wTiles: number, hTiles: number, groundLevel: number,
  round: number, cellsPerRound = 120000): {
  K: number; sliceW: number; x0: number; x1: number;
  underY0: number; underY1: number; overY0: number; overY1: number;
  gemP: number; sapP: number;
} {
  const underTiles = Math.max(1, (hTiles - Math.floor(groundLevel) - 19) * wTiles);
  const overTiles = Math.max(1, (Math.floor(groundLevel) - 11) * wTiles);
  // 每列需扫的行数（地表带 + 地下带）→ 每轮列数预算
  const rows = Math.max(1, (Math.max(0, Math.floor(groundLevel) - 11))
    + Math.max(0, hTiles - Math.floor(groundLevel) - 19));
  const sliceW = Math.max(1, Math.floor(cellsPerRound / rows));
  const K = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(wTiles / sliceW));
  const slice = round % K;
  const x0 = Math.max(0, slice * sliceW);
  const x1 = Math.min(wTiles, x0 + sliceW);
  const gemP = Math.min(1, K * 300 * 1.5e-05 * (wTiles * hTiles) / underTiles * 0.2);
  const sapP = Math.min(1, K * 300 * 3e-05 * (wTiles * hTiles) / overTiles * (1 / 20));
  return {
    K, sliceW, x0, x1,
    underY0: Math.floor(groundLevel) + 1, underY1: hTiles - 20,
    overY0: 10, overY1: Math.floor(groundLevel) - 1,
    gemP, sapP,
  };
}
```

 "old_string": "  /** 树苗生长成树。成长节奏 1:1 源自 WorldGen.UpdateWorld（:71507-71632）：\n   *  每帧在地下区均匀采 (w×h)×1.5e-05 个随机格跑 UpdateWorld_UndergroundTile，\n   *  590 宝石树苗命中再掷 1/5（:73808-73812）→ 单苗每 tick 成长概率\n   *  1.5e-05×(w×h)/((h−groundLevel−19)×w)×1/5；本扫描 300 tick 一轮，\n   *  把该概率折算成本轮命中概率（期望时长与世界尺寸/地表线无关地 ≈ 原版 60-90 分钟） */\n  private growSaplings() {\n    const st = this.world.store;\n    const grown: Array<[number, number]> = [];\n    // 只扫玩家附近 60×40 区域（原版全图均匀采样——玩家视野外同样生长，此处限域近似）\n    const ptx = Math.floor(this.player.cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(this.player.cy / TILE);\n    // 地下采样区格数（j∈[worldSurface-1, maxTilesY-20)，WorldGen.cs:71626-71630）\n    const underTiles = Math.max(1, (st.h - Math.floor(this.world.groundLevel) - 19) * st.w);\n    // 单次扫描（300 tick）的宝石苗成长概率：300 × 1.5e-05×(w×h)/underTiles × 1/5\n    const gemP = Math.min(1, 300 * 1.5e-05 * (st.w * st.h) / underTiles * 0.2);\n    // 地表橡实苗（tile 20）：地表区采样率 3e-05（:71550 num=3E-05×rate；雨天 ×1.5，\n    // :71557-71559 num5 *= 1.5），采样区 j∈[10, worldSurface-1) → 格数 ≈ (gl-11)×w；\n    // 命中掷 1/20（:72471-72475）\n    const overTiles = Math.max(1, (Math.floor(this.world.groundLevel) - 11) * st.w);\n    const sapP = Math.min(1, 300 * 3e-05 * (st.w * st.h) / overTiles * (1 / 20)\n      * (this.world.weather.raining ? TILE_BY_KEY[treeType] : 0;\n          if (!tid) continue;\n          if (growGemTree(st, new RNG((Math.random() * 1e9) | 0), x, y, tid)) {\n            st.setTile(x, y, 0);\n            this.chunks.markDirtyArea(x - 6, y - 14, x + 6, y + 2);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    for (let dy = -20; dy <= 20; dy++) {\n      for (let dx = -30; dx <= 30; dx++) {\n        const x = ptx + dx, y = pty + dy;\n        if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || st.get(x, y) !== T.SAPLING) continue;\n        // 上方 2-3 格净空才长\n        if (st.isActive(x, y - 1) || st.isActive(x, y - 2) || st.isActive(x, y - 3)) continue;\n        if (Math.random() < sapP) { // 地表采样率折算（见函数头，原版 :71550/72471）\n          grown.push([x, y]);\n        }\n      }\n    }",
 "new_string": "  /** 树苗生长成树。成长节奏数值等价源自 WorldGen.UpdateWorld 全图均匀采样\n   *  （:71507-71632）：每 tick 地下区采 (w×h)×1.5e-05 格（:71622-71631，j∈\n   *  [worldSurface-1, h-20)）、地表区采 (w×h)×3e-05 格（:71549-71555，j∈[10,\n   *  worldSurface-1)，雨天 ×1.5 :71557-71559，worldUpdateRate=1 Main.cs:734）。\n   *  本仓 300 tick 一轮 + 按列切片轮转（saplingScanPlan）：K 轮覆盖全图，\n   *  每苗每轮命中概率 = K×300×rate×(w×h)/区格数 → 单 tick 期望与原版逐点等价\n   *  （p_tick = rate×(w×h)/区格数，见 plan 函数头推导），且玩家视野外的苗同样生长\n   *  （旧实现只扫玩家附近 60×40，视野外永不生长——限域近似已废） */\n  private growSaplings() {\n    const st = this.world.store;\n    const grown: Array<[number, number]> = [];\n    const plan = saplingScanPlan(st.w, st.h, this.world.groundLevel,\n      Math.floor(this.tickCount / 300));\n    const gemP = plan.gemP;\n    const sapP = Math.min(1, plan.sapP * (this.world.weather.raining ? 1.5 : 1)); // :71557-71559\n    // 宝石树苗 590（AttemptToGrowTreeFromSapling case 590，:72924-72960）：必须地下\n    // （y>worldSurface）；frameX/54 = 宝石序号 → 对应树 583-589；growGemTree 自带\n    // 石/苔基座 + 洞穴墙 + 净空全套判定\n    const GEM_SAP = TILE_BY_KEY['v_590_gem_saplings'];\n    if (GEM_SAP) {\n      for (let x = plan.x0; x < plan.x1; x++) {\n        for (let y = plan.underY0; y < plan.underY1; y++) {\n          if (st.get(x, y) !== GEM_SAP) continue;\n          if (y <= this.world.groundLevel) continue; // 必须 underground（原版硬门）\n          if (Math.random() >= gemP) continue;       // 原版每帧采样概率折算（见函数头）\n          const ii = st.idx(x, y);\n          const kind = Math.floor(st.frameX[ii] / 54); // frame 带宽 54（placeStyle×18×fw?）\n          const treeSheet = 583 + Math.min(6, Math.max(0, kind));\n          // 树干内部帧带宽查 TREE_DEFS（growGemTree 用的带宽与放置一致）\n          const treeType = TILE_DEFS.find((d) => d.vanilla?.sheet === treeSheet)?.key;\n          const tid = treeType ? TILE_BY_KEY[treeType] : 0;\n          if (!tid) continue;\n          if (growGemTree(st, new RNG((Math.random() * 1e9) | 0), x, y, tid)) {\n            st.setTile(x, y, 0);\n            this.chunks.markDirtyArea(x - 6, y - 14, x + 6, y + 2);\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    for (let x = plan.x0; x < plan.x1; x++) {\n      for (let y = plan.overY0; y < plan.overY1; y++) {\n        if (st.get(x, y) !== T.SAPLING) continue;\n        // 上方 2-3 格净空才长\n        if (st.isActive(x, y - 1) || st.isActive(x, y - 2) || st.isActive(x, y - 3)) continue;\n        if (Math.random() < sapP) { // 地表采样率折算（见函数头，原版 :71550/72471）\n          grown.push([x, y]);\n        }\n      }\n    }"

 "old_string": "/** 物品 value 表（vanilla-shopstock.json 附带；旅行商定价用） */\nconst ITEM_VALUES = (shopstockJson as { values: Record<string, number> }).values;",
 "new_string": "/** 物品 value 表（vanilla-shopstock.json 附带；旅行商定价用） */\nconst ITEM_VALUES = (shopstockJson as { values: Record<string, number> }).values;\n\n/** 树苗扫描切片计划（growSaplings 消费）。原版 WorldGen.UpdateWorld 每 tick 在全图\n *  均匀采样（地下 (w×h)×1.5e-05 格 :71622-71631；地表 (w×h)×3e-05 格 :71549-71555，\n *  worldUpdateRate=1）——单苗每 tick 被采中概率 = rate×(w×h)/区格数。本仓 300 tick\n *  一轮、按列切片轮转（每轮预算 cellsPerRound 格，K 轮覆盖全图），每苗每轮命中概率\n *  gemP/sapP = K×300×rate×(w×h)/区格数（×590 命中再掷 1/5、20 苗命中掷 1/20），\n *  单 tick 期望 = gemP/(K×300) 与原版逐点相等——数值等价实现（等价推导登记于\n *  growSaplings 函数头），空间上不再限域玩家附近 */\nexport function saplingScanPlan(wTiles: number, hTiles: number, groundLevel: number,\n  round: number, cellsPerRound = 120000, raining = false): {\n  K: number; sliceW: number; x0: number; x1: number;\n  underY0: number; underY1: number; overY0: number; overY1: number;\n  gemP: number; sapP: number;\n} {\n  // 采样区格数（原版 y 取值域宽度 × w）：地下 j∈[worldSurface-1, h-20)，\n  // 地表 j∈[10, worldSurface-1)（WorldGen.cs:71553-71555/:71627-71629）\n  const underTiles = Math.max(1, (hTiles - Math.floor(groundLevel) - 19) * wTiles);\n  const overTiles = Math.max(1, (Math.floor(groundLevel) - 11) * wTiles);\n  // 每列需扫行数（地表带+地下带）→ 每轮列数预算 → K 轮覆盖全图\n  const rows = Math.max(1, Math.max(0, Math.floor(groundLevel) - 11)\n    + Math.max(0, hTiles - Math.floor(groundLevel) - 19));\n  const sliceW = Math.max(1, Math.floor(cellsPerRound / rows));\n  const K = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(wTiles / sliceW));\n  const slice = round % K;\n  const x0 = Math.max(0, slice * sliceW);\n  const x1 = Math.min(wTiles, x0 + sliceW);\n  return {\n    K, sliceW, x0, x1,\n    underY0: Math.floor(groundLevel) - 1, underY1: hTiles - 20,   // [gl-1, h-21]\n    overY0: 10, overY1: Math.floor(groundLevel) - 1,              // [10, gl-2]\n    // 每苗每轮命中概率（K 轮一访 × 300 tick/轮 × 原版单 tick 采样率 × 命中掷骰）\n    gemP: Math.min(1, K * 300 * 1.5e-05 * (wTiles * hTiles) / underTiles * 0.2),\n    sapP: Math.min(1, K * 300 * 3e-05 * (wTiles * hTiles) / overTiles * (1 / 20)\n      * (raining ? 1.5 : 1)),\n  };\n}"
}

1	// 实体基类与分桶管理器
2	import type { GameHooks } from './types';
3	import type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';
4	import type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';
5	
6	export abstract class Entity {
7	  id = 0;
8	  x = 0; y = 0;          // 左上角 px
9	  w = 16; h = 16;
10	  vx = 0; vy = 0;
11	  dead = false;
12	  onGround = false;
13	  hitWall = false;
14	  hitHead = false;
15	  dropThrough = false;
16	  // ---- 联机傀儡标记（v3 实体同步）：快照驱动实体，本地不跑 AI/命中判定 ----
17	  netPuppet = false;
18	  /** 联机实体号（拥有者 slot 高 8 位 + 本地 id 低 24 位；见 net/entitySyncHooks） */
19	  netId = 0;
20	  // ---- P3.2 傀儡插值缓冲（15Hz 快照 × 60TPS 渲染的平滑桥）----
21	  /** 最新权威快照位（逻辑位：接触判定用；= 房主认为的"现在"） */
22	  netAx = 0; netAy = 0;
23	  /** 插值起点（快照到达时的渲染位）与进度（1=已到目标，纯外推） */
24	  netIx = 0; netIy = 0; netIt = 1;
25	  private netSnapIvt = 4;
26	
27	  abstract fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks): void;
28	  abstract draw(r: Renderer, cam: Camera): void;
29	
30	  /** 快照到达（Game.netApplyNpcSnaps / netApplyProjSnaps 调用）：设插值目标。
31	   *  从当前渲染位连续出发（中途到包不跳变）；intervalTicks = 快照周期（15Hz=4 tick） */
32	  netSnapTo(x: number, y: number, intervalTicks = 4) {
33	    this.netIx = this.x; this.netIy = this.y;
34	    this.netAx = x; this.netAy = y;
35	    this.netIt = 0;
36	    this.netSnapIvt = Math.max(1, intervalTicks);
37	  }
38	
39	  /** 傀儡每 tick 推进：未到目标走 smoothstep 插值（一个快照周期到达），到后纯速度外推 */
40	  netPuppetStep() {
41	    if (this.netIt < 1) {
42	      this.netIt = Math.min(1, this.netIt + 1 / this.netSnapIvt);
43	      const t = this.netIt * this.netIt * (3 - 2 * this.netIt); // smoothstep
44	      this.x = this.netIx + (this.netAx - this.netIx) * t;
45	      this.y = this.netIy + (this.netAy - this.netIy) * t;
46	      return;
47	    }
48	    this.x += this.vx;
49	    this.y += this.vy;
50	  }
51	
52	  get cx() { return this.x + this.w / 2; }
53	  get cy() { return this.y + this.h / 2; }
54	  aabbOverlaps(o: Entity): boolean {
55	    return this.x < o.x + o.w && this.x + this.w > o.x && this.y < o.y + o.h && this.y + this.h > o.y;
56	  }
57	}
58	
59	/** 就地剔除 dead 实体(保持顺序,零分配) */
60	function compact(list: Entity[]): void {
61	  let w = 0;
62	  for (let r = 0; r < list.length; r++) {
63	    const e = list[r];
64	    if (!e.dead) list[w++] = e;
65	  }
66	  list.length = w;
67	}
68	
69	export class EntityManager {
70	  nextId = 1;
71	  enemies: Entity[] = [];
72	  drops: Entity[] = [];
73	  npcs: Entity[] = [];
74	  projectiles: Entity[] = [];
75	  props: Entity[] = [];   // 场景物件（墓碑等不可拾取物理实体）
76	  critters: Entity[] = []; // 小动物（无害被动生物）
77	
78	  add(e: Entity, bucket: 'enemies' | 'drops' | 'npcs' | 'projectiles' | 'props' | 'critters') {
79	    e.id = this.nextId++;
80	    this[bucket].push(e);
81	  }
82	
83	  update(dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
84	    // 就地压缩替代 6×filter(2026-08 审计 G9:每 tick 6 个新数组,60Hz 恒定分配)
85	    for (const list of [this.enemies, this.drops, this.npcs, this.projectiles, this.props, this.critters]) {
86	      for (const e of list) e.fixedUpdate(dt, game);
87	      compact(list);
88	    }
89	  }
90	
91	  clear() {
92	    this.enemies.length = 0;
93	    this.drops.length = 0;
94	    this.npcs.length = 0;
95	    this.projectiles.length = 0;
96	    this.props.length = 0;
97	    this.critters.length = 0;
98	  }
99	
100	  all(): Entity[] {
101	    return [...this.enemies, ...this.drops, ...this.npcs, ...this.projectiles, ...this.props, ...this.critters];
102	  }
103	}
104

 "content": "// 治疗弹（原版 Projectile 298 GhostHeal / 305 VampireHeal，aiStyle 52）：\n// 吸血/鬼疗命中敌怪后从命中点起飞，追踪目标玩家，抵达（距离<50 且盒相交）结算\n// 回复 floor(ai[1]) 并消亡。Projectile.AI aiStyle 52 :27114-27165 1:1：\n//   - 速度 4（num399 :27117），每 extraUpdates 次速度向目标插值：\n//     305 档 (v×15+dir)/16（:27142-27144，extraUpdates=10 :3696）；\n//     298 档 (v×100+dir)/101（:27101-27103，extraUpdates=3 :3614）\n//   - 命中门（:27122-27128）：dist<50 且弹盒与目标矩形相交\n//   - 结算（:27129-27140）：HealEffect((int)ai[1]) + statLife 上限钳制 + Kill\n// 弹体参数（SetDefaults :3610-3617 / :3692-3698）：6×6、alpha 255、tileCollide=false\n// （穿墙）、298 magic=true（已由调用点魔法门保证）。\n// 单人语义：原版鬼疗跨队友选最缺血者（ghostHeal :11424-11434），本仓无队友体系，\n// ai[0] 恒 = 本地玩家（与吸血 305 同——vampireHeal :11447 也固定 owner）。\nimport { Entity } from './Entity';\nimport type { GameHooks } from './types';\nimport type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';\nimport type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';\n\nexport class HealProj extends Entity {\n  w = 6; h = 6;\n  /** 原版弹型（305 吸血 / 298 鬼疗）——决定插值档与尾迹密度 */\n  readonly projType: 298 | 305;\n  /** ai[1] 回复量（float，结算时 (int) 取整 :27130） */\n  readonly heal: number;\n  /** 插值档：305 → (v×15+dir)/16、extraUpdates 10；298 → (v×100+dir)/101、3 */\n  private readonly lerpNum: number;\n  private readonly extraUpdates: number;\n  /** 目标玩家引用（原版 ai[0] 玩家下标；单人 = 本地玩家） */\n  private readonly target: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number;\n    hp: number; maxHp: number };\n  private life = 3600;   // 兜底寿命（原版 NewProjectile 默认 timeLeft，防永不命中滞留）\n\n  constructor(projType: 298 | 305, x: number, y: number, heal: number,\n    target: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; hp: number; maxHp: number }) {\n    super();\n    this.projType = projType;\n    this.x = x; this.y = y;\n    this.heal = heal;\n    this.target = target;\n    this.lerpNum = projType === 305 ? 15 : 100;      // :27142 / :27101\n    this.extraUpdates = projType === 305 ? 10 : 3;   // :3696 / :3614\n  }\n\n  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {\n    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');\n    if (!ctx) return;\n    // 305 红雾 / 298 青白雾（原版 dust 183 / 90 族配色近似，弹体本体 alpha 255）\n    ctx.fillStyle = this.projType === 305 ? '#D04060' : '#70F0D0';\n    ctx.beginPath();\n    ctx.arc(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2, 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);\n    ctx.fill();\n  }\n\n  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {\n    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }\n    // extraUpdates 次子步（原版每 tick extraUpdates+1 次 AI 速度更新+位移）\n    for (let u = 0; u <= this.extraUpdates; u++) {\n      const vcx = this.x + this.w / 2, vcy = this.y + this.h / 2;\n      const dx = this.target.x + this.target.w / 2 - vcx;\n      const dy = this.target.y + this.target.h / 2 - vcy;\n      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);\n      // 命中门（:27122-27128）：dist<50 且盒相交 → 结算回复并消亡\n      if (dist < 50 && this.x < this.target.x + this.target.w && this.x + this.w > this.target.x\n        && this.y < this.target.y + this.target.h && this.y + this.h > this.target.y) {\n        const n = Math.floor(this.heal);             // (int)ai[1] :27130\n        this.target.hp = Math.min(this.target.maxHp, this.target.hp + n);\n        if (n > 0) game.addDamageNumber(this.target.x + this.target.w / 2, this.target.y, n, false, '#40E080');\n        this.dead = true;\n        return;\n      }\n      // 速度 4 归一方向 + 插值趋近（:27117/:27142-27144 / :27101-27103）\n      const nx = dx / dist * 4, ny = dy / dist * 4;\n      this.vx = (this.vx * this.lerpNum + nx) / (this.lerpNum + 1);\n      this.vy = (this.vy * this.lerpNum + ny) / (this.lerpNum + 1);\n      this.x += this.vx;   // tileCollide=false——穿墙不检碰撞（:3615/:3696）\n      this.y += this.vy;\n    }\n  }\n}\n"
}

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { Player } from '../src/entities/Player';
import { Inventory } from '../src/items/Inventory';
import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/tiles';

// 黏滞 tile（Collision.StickyTiles + Player.cs:22650-22740）
const fakeHooks = (w: World) => ({
  world: w,
  spawnDrop: () => null,
  addDamageNumber: () => {},
  playSfx: () => {},
  spawnParticles: () => {},
  onInventoryChanged: () => {},
});

describe('蛛网/蜂蜜块黏滞（StickyTiles 1:1）', () => {
  it('蛛网内：下落钳 1 且 ×0.3 缓沉、水平 ×0.6/0.85 阻尼、禁跳', () => {
    const w = new World(40, 40, 42, 'web');
    const st = w.store;
    const WEB = TILE_BY_KEY['v_51_cobweb']!;
    // 20,20 放一列 4 格蛛网
    for (let y = 18; y <= 21; y++) st.setTileSilent(20, y, WEB);
    const p = new Player(20 * 16, 17 * 16, new Inventory());
    p.vy = 8; p.vx = 3; p.jumpHold = 10; // 高速下落 + 横移 + 跳跃中
    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w) as never);
    expect(p.vy).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1 * 0.3 + 0.36); // 钳 1 后 ×0.3 + 本 tick 重力
    expect(p.jumpHold).toBe(0);
    expect(p.vx).toBeLessThan(1.5); // 水平阻尼明显
  });

  it('无黏滞 tile 时不受影响', () => {
    const w = new World(40, 40, 42, 'open');
    const p = new Player(20 * 16, 17 * 16, new Inventory());
    p.vy = 8;
    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w) as never);
    expect(p.vy).toBeGreaterThan(4); // 正常重力累积

  });

  it('撕网目标 1:1（Collision.StickyTiles 列优先 cs:3399）：最左重叠列优先于更上行的网', () => {
    const w = new World(40, 40, 42, 'open');

n <= 5000; n++) travelAdjustSlotRarities(r, n);   // :1278-1299 环\n    expect(r).toEqual([1, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1]);\n  });\n});\n\n// ============ 6 植物/树苗采样（WorldGen.cs:71549-71631 数值等价） ============\ndescribe('树苗全图切片采样（密度等价）', () => {\n  it('单 tick 命中密度与原版逐点相等：gemP/(K×300) == 1.5e-05×(w×h)/区格数', () => {\n    for (const [w, h, gl] of [[4200, 1200, 350], [8400, 2400, 500], [400, 600, 180]] as const) {\n      const plan = saplingScanPlan(w, h, gl, 0);\n      const underTiles = (h - Math.floor(gl) - 19) * w;      // :71627-71629 区格数\n      expect(plan.gemP / (plan.K * 300)).toBeCloseTo(1.5e-05 * (w * h) / underTiles, 9);\n      const overTiles = (Math.floor(gl) - 11) * w;           // :71553-71555\n      expect(plan.sapP / (plan.K * 300)).toBeCloseTo(3e-05 * (w * h) / overTiles, 9);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('K 轮列切片无重叠覆盖全图（全图采样、不限域玩家附近）', () => {\n    const w = 4200, h = 1200, gl = 350;\n    let seen = 0, prevEnd = 0;\n    for (let round = 0; round < 64; round++) {\n      const plan = saplingScanPlan(w, h, gl, round);\n      expect(plan.x0).toBe(prevEnd % (w + 1));   // 连续切片\n      prevEnd = plan.x1;\n      if (plan.x0 >= plan.x1) continue;\n      seen += plan.x1 - plan.x0;\n      if (round % plan.K === plan.K - 1) { expect(seen).toBe(w); seen = 0; }\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('雨天 ×1.5（:71557-71559）', () => {\n    const dry = saplingScanPlan(4200, 1200, 350, 0, 120000, false);\n    const rain = saplingScanPlan(4200, 1200, 350, 0, 120000, true);\n    expect(rain.sapP).toBeCloseTo(dry.sapP * 1.5, 9);\n    expect(rain.gemP).toBeCloseTo(dry.gemP, 9);   // 地下不受雨影响\n  });\n});\n\n// ============ 7 吸血/鬼疗治疗弹（Projectile.cs:11404-11448 + aiStyle 52） ============\ndescribe('治疗弹飞行结算（aiStyle 52 :27114-27165）', () => {\n  const hooks = { addDamageNumber: () => {} } as never;\n\n  it('305 吸血弹：自命中点追踪飞达，回复 floor(ai[1]) 后消亡', () => {\n    const target = { x: 0, y: 0, w: 20, h: 42, hp: 50, maxHp: 100 };\n    const proj = new HealProj(305, 200, 0, 7.7, target);\n    let ticks = 0;\n    while (!proj.dead && ticks++ < 200) proj.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);\n    expect(proj.dead).toBe(true);\n    expect(target.hp).toBe(57);            // floor(7.7)=7（:27130 (int)ai[1]）\n  });\n\n  it('298 鬼疗弹档更慢（extraUpdates 3 vs 10，:3614/:3696）且同样结算', () => {\n    const mk = (type: 298 | 305) => {\n      const target = { x: 0, y: 0, w: 20, h: 42, hp: 50, maxHp: 100 };\n      const proj = new HealProj(type, 240, 0, 3.7, target);\n      let ticks = 0;\n      while (!proj.dead && ticks++ < 400) proj.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);\n      return { ticks, hp: target.hp };\n    };\n    const fast = mk(305);\n    const slow = mk(298);\n    expect(fast.hp).toBe(53);              // floor(3.7)=3\n    expect(slow.hp).toBe(53);\n    expect(slow.ticks).toBeGreaterThan(fast.ticks);\n  });\n\n  it('回复上限钳制（:27133-27136）', () => {\n    const target = { x: 0, y: 0, w: 20, h: 42, hp: 98, maxHp: 100 };\n    const proj = new HealProj(305, 150, 0, 7.7, target);\n    let ticks = 0;\n    while (!proj.dead && ticks++ < 200) proj.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);\n    expect(target.hp).toBe(100);\n  });\n\n  it('追踪方向正确：从目标右侧起飞必向左接近（速度插值 :27142-27145）', () => {\n    const target = { x: 0, y: 0, w: 20, h: 42, hp: 50, maxHp: 100 };\n    const proj = new HealProj(305, 200, 0, 5, target);\n    proj.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);\n    expect(proj.x).toBeLessThan(200);\n    expect(proj.vx).toBeLessThan(0);\n  });\n});\n"
}

 "new_string": "      // ---- 冲刺 1:1（DashMovement :20769-21265 + DoCommonDashHandle :21267-21323）----\n      // 1=忍者足具/水晶刺客（纯位移） 2=克苏鲁护盾（冲撞 30+反弹）。\n      // dashDelay 三态（:20775-20782）：0=就绪 / -1=滑行中（阻尼衰减） / >0=冷却倒计时。\n      // 三态互斥（原版 if/else-if/else 同 tick 只走一支：:20958/:20970/:21128）——\n      // 触发帧不再叠跑阻尼。触发帧单次置速（:21131 dash1=16.9 / :21162 dash2=14.5，\n      // 原版 px 值），此后靠滑行阻尼自然衰减——无恒速段（旧 dashTicks=15×12.5 近似\n      // 已废）；重力照常作用（原版 JumpMovement 不因 dashing 豁免）\n      // 克苏鲁护盾冲撞（:20784-20846）：eocDash>0 且未命中时逐帧扫敌。命中判定盒 =\n      // 位置 + 速度×0.5 外扩 4px（:20786）；伤害 30×meleeDamage 可暴击、击退 9\n      // （kbGlove ×2 / 泰坦 kbBuff ×1.5，:20800-20808）；命中 → eocDash=10、30t 冷却、\n      // 反弹 vx=-dir×9 vy=-4（:20825-20840）\n      if (eq.dash === 2 && this.eocDash > 0 && this.eocHitId < 0) {\n        const bhx = this.x + this.vx * 0.5 - 4, bhy = this.y + this.vy * 0.5 - 4;\n        const bhw = this.w + 8, bhh = this.h + 8;\n        for (const e of game.enemies()) {\n          const en = e as { id?: number; x?: number; y?: number; w?: number; h?: number; cx?: number; cy?: number;\n            dead?: boolean; hurt?: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: unknown) => boolean };\n          if (en.dead || !en.hurt || en.cx === undefined || en.cy === undefined) continue;\n          const ex = en.x ?? en.cx - 8, ey = en.y ?? en.cy - 8, ew = en.w ?? 16, eh = en.h ?? 16;\n          if (!(bhx < ex + ew && bhx + bhw > ex && bhy < ey + eh && bhy + bhh > ey)) continue;\n          const dir = this.vx < 0 ? -1 : this.vx > 0 ? 1 : this.facing; // :20809-20815\n          const crit = Math.random() * 100 < this.critChance('melee');  // :20811-20813\n          const dmg = Math.max(1, Math.round(30 * this.damageMult('melee')) * (crit ? 2 : 1));\n          const kb = 9 * (eq.kbGlove ? 2 : 1) * (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Titan) ? 1.5 : 1);\n          en.hurt(dmg, dir * kb, -4, game);\n          game.addDamageNumber(en.cx, en.cy, dmg, crit, crit ? '#FF8040' : '#FFD080');\n          this.eocDash = 10;            // :20833\n          this.eocHitId = en.id ?? -2;  // :20840（后续帧不再对同一敌结算）\n          this.dashDelay = 30;          // :20834 反弹并结束滑行\n          this.vx = -dir * 9;\n          this.vy = -4;\n          break;\n        }\n      }\n      if (this.dashDelay > 0) {\n        // 冷却段（:20958-20968）：eocDash 同步倒数，归零清冲撞命中标记\n        if (this.eocDash > 0) this.eocDash--;\n        if (this.eocDash === 0) this.eocHitId = -1;\n        this.dashDelay--;\n      } else if (this.dashDelay < 0) {\n        // 滑行阻尼（:20970-21113）：|vx|>12 → ×0.985；否则 |vx|>max(accRunSpeed,\n        // maxRunSpeed) → ×0.94；跌破档位即结束滑行（dashDelay=20）并把 vx 钳回\n        // ±maxRunSpeed（:21108-21113）。num13 = max(accRunSpeed,maxRunSpeed)\n        // （accRunSpeed=靴 6/裸 3，maxRunSpeed=3×moveSpeed）\n        const num13 = Math.max(eq.accRunSpeed > 0 ? eq.accRunSpeed : 3, 3 * eq.moveMult);\n        this.vortexStealthActive = false;  // :21106 滑行中强制显形\n        if (Math.abs(this.vx) > 12) {\n          this.vx *= 0.985;\n        } else if (Math.abs(this.vx) > num13) {\n          this.vx *= 0.94;\n        } else {\n          this.dashDelay = 20;             // :21110（dash1/2 档 num15=20）\n          this.vx = this.vx < 0 ? -num13 : num13;\n        }\n      } else if (eq.dash > 0) {\n        // 就绪段（:21128 else）：DoCommonDashHandle（:21267-21323）——双击窗收敛 +\n        // 同向二连击触发（原版此段只在 dashDelay==0 时运行：冷却/滑行期点击不登记窗）\n        if (this.dashT > 0) this.dashT--;   // :21268-21271 每帧向 0 收敛\n        else if (this.dashT < 0) this.dashT++;\n        if (this.inputX !== 0 && this.prevInputX === 0) {\n          const dir = this.inputX;\n          if (dir > 0 ? this.dashT > 0 : this.dashT < 0) {\n            this.dashT = 0;               // :21305 触发即清双击窗\n            this.dashDir = dir;\n            this.dashDelay = -1;          // :21138 进入滑行态\n            this.vx = (eq.dash === 2 ? 14.5 : 16.9) * dir;   // :21162 / :21131 单帧置速\n            // 头部将撞墙减半（:21132-21137）：身前半宽+2px 的头部点/身体中点格\n            // SolidOrSlopedTile → velocity.X /= 2\n            const wtx = Math.floor((this.cx + dir * (this.w / 2) + 2 * dir) / TILE);\n            const hty = Math.floor((this.cy + this.gravDir * (-this.h / 2) + 2 * this.gravDir) / TILE);\n            const solidOrSloped = (tx: number, ty: number): boolean =>\n              st.inBounds(tx, ty) && !!st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty)] && st.isSolid(tx, ty);\n            if (solidOrSloped(wtx, hty) || solidOrSloped(wtx, Math.floor(this.cy / TILE))) this.vx /= 2;\n            if (eq.dash === 2) { this.eocDash = 15; this.eocHitId = -1; } // :21170\n          } else {\n            this.dashT = dir * 15;        // :21316-21321 首击登记双击窗（dashTime ±15）\n          }\n        }\n      }"
}

 "new_string": "// ============ 1 魔力回复整模型（:19214-19302） ============\ndescribe('魔力回复（UpdateManaRegen :19214-19302）', () => {\n  /** 落地稳定后重置魔力状态（settle 期间蓄能/回复会污染断言基线） */\n  const reset = (p: Player, mana: number): void => {\n    p.mana = mana;\n    p.manaRegenCount = 0;\n    p.manaRegenDelay = 0;\n    p.manaAnimTicks = 0;\n  };\n\n  it('移动中基础产额：max100/mana50 → floor((33+1)×0.6×1.15)=23/tick', () => {\n    const w = mkWorld();\n    const p = mkPlayer(w);\n    p.baseMaxMana = 100;\n    settle(p, w);\n    reset(p, 50);\n    p.vx = 10;                       // 非静止（IsConsideredStandingStill :3175 |v|<0.05）\n    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w, p) as never);\n    expect(p.manaRegenCount).toBe(23);   // :19251-19268 产额公式\n    expect(p.mana).toBe(50);             // 23 < 120 不结算（:19275）\n  });\n\n  it('静止加成：+max/3 → floor(67×0.6×1.15)=46/tick', () => {\n    const w = mkWorld();\n    const p = mkPlayer(w);\n    p.baseMaxMana = 100;\n    settle(p, w);\n    reset(p, 50);\n    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w, p) as never);   // 落地静止 vx=vy=0\n    expect(p.manaRegenCount).toBe(46);\n  });\n\n  it('蓄能结算：累积 ≥120 才 +1 魔并扣 120（6×23=138 → mana51/count18）', () => {\n    const w = mkWorld();\n    const p = mkPlayer(w);\n    p.baseMaxMana = 100;\n    settle(p, w);\n    reset(p, 50);\n    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {\n      p.vx = 10;\n      p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w, p) as never);\n    }\n    expect(p.mana).toBe(51);\n    expect(p.manaRegenCount).toBe(18);  // 138-120\n  });\n\n  it('存量系数 num2：满魔 1.0 → 39/tick；空魔 0.2 → 7/tick（:19262-19268）', () => {\n    const w = mkWorld();\n    const p = mkPlayer(w);\n    p.baseMaxMana = 100;\n    settle(p, w);\n    reset(p, 100);\n    p.vx = 10;\n    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w, p) as never);\n    expect(p.manaRegenCount).toBe(39);  // floor(34×1.0×1.15)\n    reset(p, 0);\n    p.vx = 10;\n    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w, p) as never);\n    expect(p.manaRegenCount).toBe(7);   // floor(34×0.2×1.15)\n  });\n\n  it('用魔惩罚期：onManaItemUsed 后 delay=(1−0.5)×240+45 再 ×0.7=115.5，产额 0', () => {\n    const w = mkWorld();\n    const p = mkPlayer(w);\n    p.baseMaxMana = 100;\n    settle(p, w);\n    reset(p, 50);\n    p.useTime = 10;\n    p.onManaItemUsed();                // :42131-42137 动画窗内每帧重置\n    p.vx = 10;                         // 移动 → 不吃静止额外衰减\n    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w, p) as never);\n    expect(p.manaRegenDelay).toBeCloseTo(115.5 - 1, 6);  // :24378-24379 公式后 −1 衰减\n    expect(p.manaRegenCount).toBe(0);  // delay>0 → manaRegen=0（:19270-19271）\n    expect(p.mana).toBe(50);\n  });\n\n  it('魔力再生 buff：惩罚期钳 20（:19247-19250）+ 静止加成常开 + num2 恒 1', () => {\n    const w = mkWorld();\n    const p = mkPlayer(w);\n    p.baseMaxMana = 100;\n    settle(p, w);\n    reset(p, 50);\n    p.buffs.apply(BuffType.ManaRegen, 480);\n    p.useTime = 10;\n    p.onManaItemUsed();\n    p.vx = 10;\n    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w, p) as never);\n    expect(p.manaRegenDelay).toBe(20); // 113.5 → 帽 20\n    // 惩罚期过后（delay 清零）产额 = floor((34+33)×1.0×1.15)=77（num2 恒 1 :19265-19267）\n    reset(p, 50);\n    p.vx = 10;\n    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w, p) as never);\n    expect(p.manaRegenCount).toBe(77);\n  });\n\n  it('瓶中星 158：manaRegenBonus+10 / delayBonus+0.5（:9629-9632）', () => {\n    const w = mkWorld();\n    const p = mkPlayer(w);\n    p.baseMaxMana = 100;\n    settle(p, w);\n    reset(p, 50);\n    p.buffs.apply(BuffType.StarInBottle, 4800);\n    p.vx = 10;\n    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w, p) as never);\n    // floor((34+10)×0.6×1.15)=floor(30.36)=30\n    expect(p.manaRegenCount).toBe(30);\n    // delayBonus 只作用于惩罚期衰减：用魔后每 tick 多退 0.5\n    reset(p, 50);\n    p.useTime = 10;\n    p.onManaItemUsed();\n    p.vx = 10;\n    p.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, fakeHooks(w, p) as never);\n    expect(p.manaRegenDelay).toBeCloseTo(115.5 - 1 - 0.5, 6); // :19236-19237\n  });\n});"

  }

  get(x: number, y: number): number { return this.inBounds(x, y) ? this.type[this.idx(x, y)] : 0; }

  isActive(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    // 原版 nactive() = active && !inActive（Tile.cs:383-394）：已致动格视为不活跃
    return this.inBounds(x, y) && this.type[this.idx(x, y)] !== 0
      && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) === 0;
  }

  /** 原版 inActive()：致动器翻转后的"缩入地下"态（有 type 但不碰撞不阻挡） */
  isActuated(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    return this.inBounds(x, y) && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) !== 0;
  }

  isSolid(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false; // 世界外视为空（边缘自由）
    if (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) return false; // 已致动不碰撞
    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
    return !!d && d.solid;
  }

  isPlatform(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
    return !!d && d.platform;
  }

  onTileChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {
    this.listeners.push(fn);
  }

  /** 液体变化独立通道：chunk 缓存不订阅（水不入烘焙画布，无需重建），
   *  只有光照订阅（水的光衰减）——避免水流时每帧冲爆 chunk 重建队列造成卡顿 */
  private liquidListeners: Array<(x: number, y: number) => void> = [];
  onLiquidChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {
    this.liquidListeners.push(fn);
  }

  /** 唯一的写入入口（生成/导入期可绕过用 setTileSilent） */
  setTile(x: number, y: number, type: number, frameX = 0, frameY = 0) {

1958:  spawn(playerX: number, playerY: number, rng: RNG, viewHalfW: number, viewHalfH: number): Enemy | null {
  spawn(playerX: number, playerY: number, rng: RNG, viewHalfW: number, viewHalfH: number): Enemy | null {
    this.pX = Math.floor(playerX / TILE);
    this.pY = Math.floor(playerY / TILE);
    this.dayTime = this.world.clock.isDay;
    this.hardMode = !!this.world.flags.hardMode;
    // SetSpawnFlags L321：noWorms = 玩家所站格带房屋墙（房屋内不出蠕虫）
    this.noWorms = this.world.store.inBounds(this.pX, this.pY)
      && WALL_HOUSE.has(this.world.store.wall[this.world.store.idx(this.pX, this.pY)]);
    this.skyMob = false;
    this.spawnSpider = false;
    // 邻近旗标每尝试复位(原版每次 TrySpawnAnNPC 全新置旗;
    // 泄漏会导致碰过大理石后全图永远出蛇发女妖)
    this.nearMarble = false;
    this.nearGranite = false;
    this.spawnUndergroundDesert = false;
    if (!this.findSpawnTile(rng, viewHalfW, viewHalfH)) return null;
    // TrySpawnAnNPC L220：CheckNotSpawningOnScreen 像素级二次校验（画面内/贴屏拒绝）
    if (!this.checkNotSpawningOnScreen(playerX, playerY, viewHalfW, viewHalfH)) return null;
    // GetProperGroundSpawnTileTypeAndWallType（L5789-5792）：tile = 落脚格自身 type，
    // wall = 落脚格【上方一格】的墙（skyMob 天空怪落点无实心面 → tile=0）
    const st = this.world.store;
    const groundType = this.skyMob ? 0 : st.type[st.idx(this.spawnTileX, this.spawnTileY)];
    const spawnWallType = this.spawnTileY > 0
      ? st.wall[st.idx(this.spawnTileX, this.spawnTileY - 1)] : 0;
    // PostCheckChosenSpawnTile（L922-927）：地牢内刷怪点必须踩地牢砖且上方格有墙。
    // 原版失败 = TrySpawnAnNPC return false（L227-230）——整帧放弃，不回选点循环换点重试

## 4. 友好轮削减 — `src/world/spawn/VanillaSpawner.ts`
- **改前**：`friendlyHit()` 只置旗，注释声称"统一 0.6"但一行乘法都没有（假合规）。
- **改后**：地狱侧命中轮 `maxSpawns×0.5`（:724/:741/:757，未命中轮只放宽 rate）；地表侧命中轮 `×0.6`（:782/:805/:821）；**town≥3 地表无条件 ×0.6**（:830，专家例外轮也削）。墓地段（:770-778 等）维持未实装登记。

## 5. 旅行商人 — `src/core/Game.ts`（导出 `travelAdjustSlotRarities`）
- **改前**：60 次上限 + tries>40/20 三段渐降近似；首件恒有。
- **改后**：逐槽阈值降档 4200→4700（>1 才降，Chest.cs:919-947）；首件环 **仅 hardMode**（:1276 门核对）+ `while num2<5000` + 每轮先降档 + minimumRarity=2；主件环无上限（原版同，挂 100000 防死循环护栏）；末件画环 `while<5000` + 降档；`rarity` 与 array 同引用 → 降档跨环持久（:1283-1285/:1310）；num 掷骰先于首件环（RNG 消费序 1:1）。

## 6. 植物/树苗限域 — `Game.ts`（导出 `saplingScanPlan`）
- **改前**：只扫玩家附近 60×40（视野外苗永不生长）。
- **改后**：全图按列切片轮转（每轮 120k 格预算、K 轮覆盖全图），每苗每轮命中概率 `K×300×rate×(w×h)/区格数×命中掷` → **单 tick 期望与 WorldGen.UpdateWorld（:71549-71631）逐点相等**（这是唯一允许的等价项，等价式登记在两处函数头）；雨天 ×1.5（:71557-71559）。

## 7. 吸血/鬼疗 — 新实体 `src/entities/HealProj.ts` + `Game.applyLifeSteal`
- **改前**：命中直回当前玩家（近似登记）。
- **改后**：命中点发射治疗弹（305 吸血 :11447 / 298 鬼疗 :11436），初速 (0,0)、ai[1]=float 回复量；aiStyle 52（:27114-27165）1:1——速度 4、`(v×15+dir)/16` 插值（**298/305 同档**，:27142-27145）、extraUpdates 305=10/298=3（:3696/:3614）、命中门 dist<50+盒相交、`(int)ai[1]` 回复+上限钳、穿墙（tileCollide=false）。跨队友选最缺血（:11424-11434）按任务书允许的单人语义取本地玩家。

## 验证
- **新测试** `tests/a-batch1.test.ts`：**24 例全绿**（魔力 7 / 冲刺 5 / 沙靴 1 / 友好轮 2 / 商人 2 / 采样 3 / 治疗弹 4），含原版公式算例（23/46/39/7 每 tick、delay 115.5、帽 20、16.9→8.45→×0.985→20t 冷却、5.2→9.1、×0.5/×0.6 前后、4200/4201/4701 阈值、密度等价式、floor(heal) 结算）。
- **tsc**：本批改动 **0 新增错误**。剩余错误全部来自并行 WIP（星云 booster 重构、呼吸系统重构、hell-background 私有字段等）。
- **全量**：1081/1085 通过。4 失败已隔离确认非本批：`caves-checkpoint`×2（corruption pass 分歧——他人正在改 CorruptionPass/GenState 等）、`dungeon-spawn`（`spawn()` 不经 `getSpawnRate`，本批改动不可达；该测试注释自带"genRand 位移→地牢几何漂移"的今日漂移记录）、`hive` 超时（世界生成耗时）。相关既有测试（spawn-friendly-cycle/equip-stats/cobweb/lifesteal-batch/vi-weapons）40 例全绿。
- **附带必要修复**：`Game.ts` 魔法武器分支残留的 `player.onManaSpent()` 调用——该方法已被并行星云重构删除（改 booster 掉落拾取链），移除该死调用（否则 tsc 挂）；未改其他无关代码。
